## How this is verified

```console
$ python scripts/verify_improvements.py
$ python scripts/count_improvements.py
$ magent improvements --verify
```

The verifier fails the build when any reference is missing, any symbol lacks a
docstring, any identifier is duplicated, or the total drops below the target.
